## 2026-11-11 — UEFA Champions League 2000-01 match archive (PLAYER EVALUATION INCOMPLETE)
- Created `data/ucl-2000-01.json`: 32 first-group participants, 16 second-group evaluated clubs, 12 group tables, 157 match scores (144 group + 13 knockout), 449 match goals. Recomputed all W/D/L/GF/GA/points from recorded scores and matched 12 RSSSF standings.
- Added 33 traceable player leads: Bayern and Valencia's 22 final starters, plus 11 other confirmed scorers. Five evidence-led provisional tiers (Kahn S, Raúl S, Figo S, Effenberg A+, Mendieta A+) are DRAFT and user-unapproved; 28 other candidate tiers, positional rankings and season-long XI/reserve determinations remain evidence_pending. 11 Bayern birthdates from DFB, remaining birthdays unknown where not cross-verified. Final lineups alone do not establish representative season-long starters.
- Match dates for 144 group fixtures remain null; knockout fixture dates and 2001-05-23 final are sourced. No fabricated starts, minutes, assists or unsupported DOBs. `bestXi` stays empty.
- Updated UCL browser headings/winner to use season JSON rather than 1999-00 hardcoded strings, and added penalty-shootout label. Pending CI and production UI verification. Provisional tier commit: `98c9c3d5bd7f085699056ae374db610cbe92379f`.
- Sources: [UEFA 2000-01 clubs](https://www.uefa.com/uefachampionsleague/history/seasons/2000/clubs/), [UEFA final report](https://www.uefa.com/newsfiles/461370.pdf), [RSSSF 2000-01](https://www.rsssf.org/ec/ec200001.html), [DFB 2000-01 Bayern squad](https://datencenter.dfb.de/en/competitions/champions-league/seasons/2000-2001/teams/bayern-munich).
- Main data commit: `d417e775604bbefe8375f26c1c259513abf8533b`. Interface commits: `d5d91a0a3c2cf8ce3a73f6fa901601898be80065`, `1f52f61530bcb1b7718dcf3a4989ee9ce8c18701`.

Date: 2026-11-01  
Repository: `wjdduddlf112/footballarch`, branch `main`  
Deployment target: https://wjdduddlf112.github.io/footballarch/  
Status: **24 tracked season datasets; 1682/3222 provisional player tier proposals; 1540 unrated. PL 1999-00 128/128, PL 2000-01 141/141, PL 2001-02 163/163, PL 2002-03 132/132 and PL 2003-04 105/105 and PL 2004-05 150/150 and PL 2005-06 142/142 and PL 2006-07 149/149 and PL 2007-08 170/170 and PL 2008-09 122/122 DRAFT tier/rank entries.**

## Scope and method

- Selectively imported only candidate `players` and their retained source/review details from five historical branches. Did **not** merge branches wholesale or replace modern main league tables, emblems, UI design, title/champion labels, or archived European participants.
- Where a standalone season file was absent, created it with **active main standings archive** values, league club IDs and European entrant mappings. For existing files, preserved main's `clubs`, `qualifiers`, `standings` and `bestXi`.
- Preserved La Liga 1999-00 (148) and 2000-01 (132) provisional tier + positional rank selections. The old La Liga 2000-01 branch contained 120 candidates and was intentionally not used over main.
- All imported players are pending approval; unset tier/rank remains `null`. Do not interpret an imported candidate as an individually verified starter or official positional classification.
- 2022-23 La Liga demonstration roster is outside this evaluation scope.
